## High-Angle Mediterranean Editorial with Hyper-Realistic Anatomy

## Romantic Overhead Shot with Heart Balloons

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A hyper-realistic 8k high-angle overhead shot, captured with a wide-angle lens, presents a romantic scene of a couple lying on a light-colored wooden floor. The lighting is soft and natural, possibly from a window, creating gentle highlights on the balloons and subtle shadows on the floor, contributing to a cinematic atmosphere. The female subject, uploaded face as reference, is positioned on her back, wearing a black crop top, light blue denim shorts, and a white unbuttoned shirt draped over her. Her left arm is bent with her hand near her head, and her right arm is gently extended towards the male. The male subject, uploaded face as reference, is also on his back, wearing a light grey t-shirt and light blue distressed denim jeans. His left arm is bent, hand near the female's head, and his right arm is relaxed. They are lying close together, heads touching, looking into each other's eyes with soft, affectionate smiles. The entire floor around and between them is densely decorated with a multitude of shiny red heart-shaped foil balloons of varying sizes, interspersed with several white round latex balloons, and countless small red and white rose petals, creating a highly detailed texture and vibrant color contrast against the light wood. The composition is a full-body shot, showcasing the couple and the extensive, immersive decorations.
```

## Gen Z Valentine's Day Bedroom Scene Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a hyper-realistic, trendy Gen Z Valentine’s Day scene inside a cozy, aesthetic bedroom during late afternoon golden hour.
The room has soft pastel walls, fairy lights, heart-shaped wall décor, polaroid photos, plush pillows, and scattered fashion magazines. Natural sunlight streams through sheer curtains, casting warm golden light across the space.
A stylish young couple sits casually on a messy bed covered with white and blush blankets.
The man wears relaxed streetwear — oversized hoodie, loose jeans, layered chains, soft curls slightly messy.
The woman wears an oversized pink sweater with knee-high socks, natural dewy makeup, glossy lips, and soft waves in her hair.
She holds a phone up as if filming a “Get Ready With Me – Valentine Edition” video while he leans in from behind, smiling playfully at the camera. They look relaxed, flirty, and authentic — candid but cinematic.
Heart-shaped balloons rest on the floor, and a glowing pink LED sign reading “Be Mine” is mounted on the wall.
Lighting: warm natural golden hour light + soft fairy light glow.
Lens: 35mm lifestyle lens, shallow depth of field, creamy background blur.
Style: ultra-detailed, soft skin texture, cozy influencer aesthetic, TikTok vibe, romantic but modern, candid editorial photography.
```

## Sultry Studio Fashion Photo Prompt

> Moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A studio fashion photo of a blonde young Caucasian woman in her early–mid 20s, slim and fit, standing confidently in a stylized school setting. She has a fair lightly sun-kissed complexion, oval face with sharp jawline, almond dark-brown eyes with dramatic winged eyeliner and long lashes, full glossy nude-pink lips slightly parted, contoured cheeks, defined brows, and a sultry, intense gaze at the camera. Her long straight blonde hair is sleek, middle-parted, and falls over her shoulders.
She wears a grey short-sleeve cropped collared shirt left unbuttoned, a white bralette, a loosely draped light-blue striped necktie, a very short low-rise grey pleated mini skirt, white slouched knee-high socks, and glossy black knee-high pointed-toe boots with a chunky heel. She stands tall facing forward, hips slightly right, legs shoulder-width apart, right arm down, left fingertips touching a white desk.
The scene is shot in crisp editorial studio style (2:3 ratio) with bright direct flash from front-left creating hard shadows on the wall. Background includes white school lockers with cute stickers (heart, lips, bow, apple, daisy), a low white drawer unit, a white desk with a pastel pink rotary phone, a smooth white floor, and a visible rolling chair base behind her leg. Confident, sultry vibe.
```

## Retro Art Deco Suit Editorial

> Sinematik, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Shot on a Hasselblad 907X 50C with an XCD 90mm f/2.5 lens, aperture f/2.8 shallow depth of field emphasizes the precision fit of the suit while the background dissolves into painterly color patches; captured in 16-bit RAW with high dynamic range and soft film grain to preserve wool texture and the glow of colored light without "harsh" digital sharpness. Frame a tight portrait cropped to mid-chest, at eye level, with a slight three-quarter turn; the model is offset from center so the stained-glass pattern reads in the negative space. The pose is calm and composed: shoulders open, posture confident but without tension; one hand neatly adjusts the tie knot or collar line, the other is in the trouser pocket, slightly opening the jacket and revealing the layered styling. Expression restrained and thoughtful; the gaze passes just past the lens, as if distant music is playing. Outfit a man in a suit with a retro accent: a structured double-breasted wool blazer with sharp lapels, high-waisted pleated trousers, a white shirt and narrow tie, polished Oxfords, and a minimal pocket square. Location a retro Art Deco hall with dark wood, brass, and a tall stained-glass window projecting jewel-like geometry onto the suit and background. Light warm practical fill from one side and colored stained-glass light from the other, cinematic contrast, "stained-glass" shadows. Result -retro editorial: graphic, refined, expensive, and timeless
```

## Cinematic Gourmet Burger Explosion Prompt (Code Style)

> Sinematik, stüdyo ışıklı 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
import physics_engine as cinematic
from studio_lighting import RimLight, SpotLight

class GourmetBurger:
    def __init__(self):
        self.state = "assembled"
        self.ingredients = [
            "toasted_brioche_bun", 
            "wagyu_patty", 
            "molten_cheddar", 
            "crispy_lettuce", 
            "heirloom_tomato",
            "secret_sauce_droplets"
        ]

    def trigger_explosion(self):
        """
        Executes the slow-motion deconstruction effect.
        """
        self.state = "mid-air_burst"
        
        for item in self.ingredients:
            # Applying physics for the "flying" effect
            item.apply_force(direction="outward", velocity="slow_motion")
            item.render_texture(quality="hyper_detailed")
            
        # Suspends liquid particles in 3D space
        cinematic.suspend_fluids(type="sauce", density="high_viscosity")

# Environment Configuration
scene = cinematic.Scene(background="dark_studio_charcoal")
scene.add_lighting([
    RimLight(intensity=0.8, color="warm_gold"),
    SpotLight(target="patty_texture", focus="ultra_sharp")
])

# Execute the render
burger = GourmetBurger()
burger.trigger_explosion()

render_output = scene.capture(style="cinematic_advertising", realistic=True)
```

## Sadia Sink Queen of Hearts Fashion Portrait

> Lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
High-fidelity, true-to-source facial replication. Maintain 100% of the original person's facial likeness and identity from the provided user image. The user's original photo is for identity reference ONLY. You MUST NOT alter, enhance, or stylize the subject's facial structure in any way. This includes, but is not limited to: do not change the shape of the cheeks, nose, eyes, or jawline. Avoid any 'doll-like' or idealized features. The replication of the face must be perfect. You MUST replace the original pose, clothing, and background with the new style described below. Apply the following style:

Apply a slender, elegant body build to the subject. The image is a high-concept fashion studio portrait with a "Queen of Hearts" playing card theme, presented in a portrait orientation with a 4:5 aspect ratio. The subject has long, wavy brunette hair that cascades loosely down their back. The subject is captured in a medium shot, standing with their body angled away from the camera, twisting their torso to look back over their left shoulder directly at the lens. The pose is contained and elegant, with arms crossed over the chest, hugging the bodice. The subject is wearing a stunning, deep crimson red strapless gown. The most critical stylistic element is the manipulation of the dress's fabric: massive layers of sheer, semi-translucent red chiffon or organza float and billow outwards behind the subject, sculpted to form a perfect, voluminous heart shape that dominates the frame. The fabric catches the light, showing texture, folds, and varying degrees of opacity, ranging from deep ruby in the shadows to bright cherry red where the light hits the sheer layers. The subject also wears matching matte red opera-length gloves that extend past the elbows, blending seamlessly with the red gown. The lighting is soft, diffused, high-key studio lighting that illuminates the subject evenly and creates soft shadows, emphasizing the three-dimensionality of the heart-shaped fabric. The background is a clean, solid white seamless backdrop that provides strong contrast to the red subject. A specific graphic element is present: in the top left corner, a large red serif capital letter "Q" is positioned directly above a red heart suit symbol, mimicking the index of a playing card. The overall aesthetic is romantic, polished, and editorial.
```

## 9-Panel Editorial Beauty Grid for Lipstick Commercial

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use only the female face provided by the user.
The facial identity must remain 100% identical in all frames:
same facial proportions, same eyes, nose, lips, jawline, cheekbones.
No face swap drift, no identity change between panels.
No stylization of the face.
No plastic skin.
Natural skin texture with visible pores and realistic makeup texture.

COMPOSITION:
Create a 3x3 grid layout (nine images total), perfectly aligned.
Each panel is square, evenly spaced, identical margins.
Editorial beauty campaign style.
High-end cosmetics advertising look.

BACKGROUND:
Solid deep red background (#B11226 style tone).
Same red color across all 9 panels.
No gradients, no texture on background.

OUTFIT & ACCESSORIES (CONSISTENT):
- Blue tailored blazer
- White shirt
- Red necktie
- Red acetate eyeglasses
- Hair pulled back into a sleek ponytail
- Red manicure (hands + nails match lipstick tone)

MAKEUP:
Bold red lipstick (creamy, high-pigment).
Clean eyeliner.
Soft glow on skin.
No over-smoothing.
Lip texture clearly visible.

PANEL-BY-PANEL DETAILS (MUST MATCH):

TOP ROW:
1-Smiling portrait, holding lipstick horizontally near mouth, teeth visible.
2-Side angle, eyes closed, applying lipstick directly to lips.
3-Close portrait, looking sideways, lipstick held vertically near cheek.

MIDDLE ROW:
4-Laughing expression, both hands lifting the red glasses, lipstick visible.
5-Extreme close-up of lips only, lipstick touching teeth, ultra-detailed texture.
6-Side profile, playful expression.

BOTTOM ROW:
7-Hands-only shot: two lipsticks crossed in front of chest, red nails visible.
8-Three-quarter portrait, lipstick near lips, confident gaze.
9-Extreme close-up of eye behind red glasses, eyebrow and skin texture visible.

PRODUCT:
Black lipstick tube with minimal white branding.
Brand text sharp and readable.
Same product in all panels.

LIGHTING:
Studio lighting, soft but contrasty.
Even light across all panels.
Subtle highlights on skin and lips.
No harsh shadows.

QUALITY & REALISM:
Ultra high resolution.
Editorial magazine quality.
Perfect focus.
No blur.
No AI artifacts.

IMPORTANT RULES:
- The face MUST be exactly the uploaded face in every panel.
- Expression may change, identity must NOT.
- Do NOT cartoonize.
- Do NOT stylize the face.
- Do NOT change outfit or colors.
- Only poses and crop change per panel.

STYLE:
Luxury cosmetics campaign.
Hyper-realistic.
Nano Banana Pro Edit.
Ultra clean composite.
```

```
  },
  "common_elements": {
    "subject": "[e.g., Young woman with {argument name=\"hair description\" default=\"long dark hair\"}, {argument name=\"skin tone\" default=\"fair skin\"}]",
    "face_constraint": "Preserve original face structure and proportions exactly from reference",
    "lighting_and_style": "Direct flash or golden hour, high contrast, vintage/cinematic aesthetic, saturated colors, hyper-realistic textures, 8K"
  },
  "frames": [
    {
      "frame_id": 1,
      "position": "{argument name=\"frame 1 position\" default=\"Top Left\"}",
      "visual_prompt": "{argument name=\"frame 1 visual prompt\" default=\"Low-angle shot, subject perched on object, leg extended dramatically toward camera, sharp foreground texture\"}",
      "pose_details": "[e.g., Leaning back, looking away casually]"
    },
    {
      "frame_id": 2,
      "position": "[e.g., Top Right]",
      "visual_prompt": "[e.g., High-angle shot looking down, hand on prop, wide lens distortion]",
      "pose_details": "[e.g., Seated, head tilted up]"
    }
  ],
  "negative": ["blurry", "plastic skin", "distorted face", "low res", "cartoon", "artifacts"]
}
```

```
Use uploaded image (face+shoulders) — preserve likeness exactly; top-down 4:5 close-up of subject lying on densely layered, readable newspapers (folded paper in hands headline "Valentine's Day"); hair fanned; tailored red blazer over white shirt with tiny red "ImagineArt" + heart; scattered deep-red roses, loose petals, open artisanal chocolate box near hands with assorted chocolates (one truffle bitten revealing creamy filling), loose chocolates, crumbs, cocoa dusting and faint cocoa speck on fingertips; two red heart foil balloons at top corners; heart-shaped gobo/flash across face & half body; subject wears heart-shaped chocolate-frame glasses with pink translucent lenses (eyes visible); pin-sharp face, hands, main chocolates/rose cluster with creamy bokeh (85mm/100mm, f/1.8–2.8), ISO100–200, ~1/125s, full-frame, 4K+; warm top-left softbox + subtle warm rim from right; Kodak-Portra warm toning, rich saturated reds, slight film grain; microdetail: hair strands, fabric weave, skin pores, petal veins, chocolate gloss/speculars, tiny melted gloss bead, cocoa powder texture, crumbs, faint foil fingerprint; subtle imperfections: one slightly crushed petal, small cocoa smudge on newspaper edge; ultra-realistic magazine-editorial — no added text or watermark.
```

## Editorial 3x3 Photo Grid of Intimate Portraits

> Sinematik, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Editorial 3x3 photo grid in a clean soft beige studio. Character (matches reference 100%) wearing lightweight dark navy shirt, ivory trousers, barefoot for raw simplicity. Lighting: large diffused key light directly front-right, silver reflector left, subtle rim from top. Shots to include: 1. extreme close-up of lips + cheekbone with blurred hand partially covering (85mm, f/1.8, razor-thin DOF); 2. tight crop on eyes looking into lens with reflection of light strip visible (85mm, f/2.0); 3. black & white close portrait resting chin on fist, face filling frame (50mm, f/2.2); 4. over-shoulder shot, blurred foreground fabric curtain framing half face (85mm, f/2.0); 5. very close frontal with hands overlapping face, light streak across eyes (50mm, f/2.5); 6. tight angled portrait showing hair falling into eyes, soft-focus background (85mm, f/2.2); 7. crop of hands touching jawline, eyes cropped out (50mm, f/3.2, detail-focused); 8. half-body seated sideways on low cube, head turned sharply away, blurred foreground (35mm, f/ 4.5); 9. intense close-up of profile with single tear-like water droplet, cinematic light slice across (85mm, f/ 1.9). Angles: mostly tight headshots with slight high/low tilts, maintaining variation. Capture RAW, professional muted grade, smooth tonal contrast, subtle cinematic grain. Mood: intimate, introspective, character-led editorial minimalism with delicate use of fabric as prop.
```

## Hyper-Realistic High-Fashion Squat Portrait in Stone Corridor

```
Hyper-realistic high-fashion vertical portrait (3:4) of a young woman (20–25, Caucasian/European) with a warm medium-tan bronzed skin tone and a slender, fit body. She has shoulder-length bronde hair (dark blonde/light brown with blonde highlights), straight and silky with curtain bangs below the eyebrows.
Face: neutral, confident, subtly seductive fashion gaze with direct eye contact. Almond-shaped dark brown eyes with smoky eyeliner, mascara, and soft brown eyeshadow. Groomed arched dark-blonde/light-brown eyebrows. Straight symmetrical nose, high sculpted cheekbones with warm bronzer, and full plump lips with a defined cupid’s bow in matte nude-mauve/brownish-pink.
Outfit: oversized black leather biker jacket with silver zippers and natural creases, black fitted matte bodysuit, bare smooth legs, and brown leather high-heeled mules with a single wide strap. Accessories include thick gold hoop earrings, a large structured brown leather bucket/tote bag on the left shoulder.
Pose: deep squat, knees fully bent, weight on feet, hands relaxed between knees, body angled slightly right while the face looks forward.
Location: classical European stone corridor with repeating arches and a vaulted ceiling, wrought-iron gates or doors at the sides. Soft diffused natural daylight from the open end behind the subject.
Camera/style: low angle at eye level with the squatting subject, shallow-to-medium depth of field with sharp focus on the subject and softly blurred arches, cinematic high-fashion editorial look with muted earth tones (browns, blacks, greys, beige).
```

## Moody Paris Street Editorial Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic moody Paris street editorial portrait, shot outside a vintage shopfront at dusk/overcast twilight, film-scanned look. A young woman (early–mid 20s), fair skin, slim build, shoulder-length light brown/dark blonde hair with a soft center/loose part, slightly tousled ends. Expression: calm, distant, slightly tired gaze; lips relaxed, almost pouting; eyes looking straight at camera with subtle softness. Pose: standing close to the shop entrance, shoulders slightly forward, body angled 3/4; one hand tucked into the coat pocket (hand visible at pocket opening), the other arm relaxed inside the oversized coat. She is framed from mid-thigh to a little above head, vertical portrait.

Wardrobe: oversized brown leather/bomber-style coat with a worn matte sheen, slightly wrinkled texture; very large fit with dropped shoulders. Collar: thick black shearling/faux-fur collar folded up around the neck. No visible logos, no jewelry emphasis.

Location / Background details:

•She stands at the edge of a dark green/black painted wooden doorframe with aged patina; the frame runs vertically on the right side of the image.

•Behind her is a large shop window with reflective glass showing faint street reflections (trees/sky shapes), but interior objects remain visible.

•At the very top of the window, large curved cream lettering reads: “VINS DE PROPRIÉTÉS” (French text, arched across the glass).

•Inside the shop window, a botanical poster is visible on the left side (green leaves illustration), slightly blurred due to depth/reflection.

•Lower left inside the window: a display area with dark surfaces and small objects (bottles/produce-like shapes), dimly lit.

•On the far right edge, partially inside the frame, a black menu/chalkboard with small white writing is visible (text not fully readable), aligned vertically near the doorframe.

Lighting: natural ambient street light, soft and diffused; gentle highlights on cheekbones and nose; shadows are deep but not crushed. No direct flash. The shop interior glows faintly warm compared to the cooler exterior.

Color / Tone / Aesthetic: muted brown, olive-green, charcoal palette; cinematic, slightly desaturated; subtle warm sundayfunday undertone in skin and shop interior; overall dark, cozy, rainy-winter vibe (even if no rain visible). Film grain present, slight halation on highlights, mild vignette, soft contrast roll-off. Background softly blurred but readable; subject sharpest point is face and coat collar. Cooks. 

Composition / Camera: vertical portrait (approx 4:5 feel), subject centered slightly left; window text occupies upper third; doorframe and menu board anchor the right third; medium shot at eye level, ~50mm perspective, shallow-to-moderate depth of field, realistic lens softness toward edges.

Surface / python Texture cues: aged paint on doorframe, smudged window reflections, leather coat creases, fur collar fibers visible, fine pores and natural skin texture
```

```
{
  "positive_prompt": "A high-fashion editorial photograph, captured from a low angle, showing a woman with the likeness of the person in the reference image. She is crouching elegantly, wearing a voluminous, textured golden-orange shaggy fur coat that billows around her. The main focus is on her bare legs and beautifully detailed feet in white strappy sandals, positioned prominently in the foreground. Her toenails have a clean pedicure.   "parameters": {
    "face_restoration": true,
    "face_reference_strength": 0.9,

    "num_inference_steps": 30
  }
}
```

## Quad-Pose Studio Lookbook Grid with Identity Preservation

> Minimal, lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "task_type": "studio_fashion_editorial_lookbook_quad_pose",
      "language": "en",
      "priority": "highest",
      "version": "v1.1_IVORY_CHIFFON_HALTER_LOOKBOOK_GRID_FACE_MATCH"
    },
    "input": {
      "mode": "image_to_image",
      "reference_image_usage": "high",
      "preserve_identity": true,
      "preserve_facial_features": true,
      "notes": "Use the provided reference image as the facial identity anchor. The model’s FACE must closely match the reference (very similar). Keep the overall lookbook grid style and studio setup. Do NOT change the person into someone else."
    },
    "output": {
      "aspect_ratio": "2:3",
      "resolution": "ultra_high",
```

## Emerald Green Satin Dress Fashion Editorial

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Young woman with long light brown hair styled in a sleek high ponytail, a few soft strands gently framing her face, wearing an elegant {argument name="dress color" default="emerald green"} midi dress crafted from luxurious satin or silk that catches the light with a subtle luminous sheen. The dress features a deep V-neck wrap front that enhances the silhouette, short softly puffed sleeves adding a romantic touch, and a perfectly cinched waist defined by a matching tie belt that creates a flattering hourglass shape. The skirt falls gracefully below the knees in a flowy, gathered design, moving naturally with every step, with an asymmetrical high slit on one side that reveals a glimpse of her leg and adds a refined yet bold edge.

She stands gracefully outside a sleek modern building with clean architectural lines and glass panels reflecting the daylight, giving a sophisticated fashion event atmosphere. She wears delicate gold strappy heeled sandals that complement the rich emerald tone of the dress, along with minimal elegant jewelry for a polished finish. Her makeup is natural and glowing—soft foundation, subtle contour, lightly flushed cheeks, neutral eyeshadow, defined lashes, and a muted rose lip color. Her expression is soft yet confident, poised and effortlessly stylish.

Photorealistic, ultra-detailed, sharp focus, realistic skin texture, natural daylight illumination, cinematic depth, editorial fashion photography style, 85mm lens look, shallow depth of field, luxury event vibe, high resolution, crisp fabric texture, subtle fabric folds, natural body proportions, balanced composition.
```

## Cinematic Product Video for Luxury Perfume

> Sinematik, minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a premium cinematic product video using the uploaded image as the exact visual reference.

SCENE SETUP:
A perfume bottle stands centered on a matte black cube pedestal.
The bottle is fully enclosed inside a transparent glass dome.
Soft blue fog is contained inside the glass dome, surrounding the bottle.
Background remains dark, minimal, and moody.

CAMERA:
Camera is locked in position.
No zoom.
No pan.
No rotation.
Same framing throughout the entire video.

ACTION:
The scene begins completely still.
Fog remains contained inside the glass dome.
The perfume bottle does not move.

A feminine hand slowly enters the frame from above.
The hand gently grips the top handle of the glass dome.
The movement is smooth, controlled, and elegant.

As the glass dome is lifted upward:
– The fog escapes naturally from inside the glass.
– Fog spills downward and outward in slow, fluid motion.
– The fog spreads into the surrounding space, filling the frame softly.
– The perfume bottle remains perfectly still on the pedestal.

FOG BEHAVIOR:
Fog disperses realistically due to gravity and air flow.
No explosions.
No sudden bursts.
No fast motion.
Smooth cinematic diffusion only.

LIGHTING:
Soft, low-key lighting.
No added lights.
No flares.
No glow effects.
Natural reflections on glass only.

STYLE:
Luxury
Minimal
Cinematic
High-end perfume ad aesthetic
No text
No transitions
No sound effects unless subtle ambient atmosphere is supported
```

## Urban Fashion Collage with Sydney Sweeney Identity Preservation

> Arayüz tasarımı üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a high-resolution (64K, DSLR style) fashion collage using the submitted image as the sole identity reference, preserving 100% of the facial identity exactly as it is—same facial structure, proportions, skin tone, age, expression, eyes, nose, mouth, jawline, and overall identity, without any embellishment, stylization, regeneration, reinterpretation, or modification of the face.
The final image is a vertically divided urban fashion collage with three candid street-style photos on a contemporary European city street, featuring cobblestone sidewalks, historic architecture, neutral facades, and a modern, artistic, and vibrant atmosphere.
The subject appears confident, playful, and slightly rebellious, wearing modern bold female fashion consisting of a statement crop top or corset top, a sleek high-waisted mini skirt or tailored bold trousers, a striking oversized blazer or leather jacket, fashionable heeled boots or bold sneakers, stylish accessories, a small shoulder bag, and visible white wireless headphones in some frames.
The left panel shows a reflection in a mirror through glass, with the model holding a smartphone, superimposed on subtle urban reflections.
The upper right panel is a photo taken from above, looking down, as she leans toward the camera with a playful pout and natural perspective distortion.
The lower right panel is a close-up selfie where she looks directly at the camera while holding an iced coffee in a clear plastic cup, with her headphones visible, illuminated by soft natural light.
Overlay a minimalist, semi-transparent music player interface floating in the center of the collage, displaying a Linkin Park track with title, progress bar, and play/pause controls, harmoniously integrated into a lifestyle aesthetic depicting a day in the life, focusing on music.
Use only natural light, soft shadows, realistic highlights, a discreet earthy palette (shades of brown, gray, and warm yellow), subtle grain, and a slightly desaturated editorial color gradation, resulting in a modern and realistic editorial style typical of Instagram.
```

```
A surrealist, highly photorealistic upper body portrait of {argument name="subject" default="a young man/woman with specific features"}, standing in a sunny city street. Five identical heads of the same person are stacked vertically like a tall totem pole rising from the shoulders. Each head has a different size, unique facial expression, and is tilted at a different angle. Strong, directional sunlight hits the figures from the side, casting deep, realistic volumetric shadows between the stacked heads and onto the torso, defining their three-dimensional form. Highly detailed skin texture is visible, avoiding a plastic look. The urban architectural background is slightly blurred with realistic bokeh, showing depth. Cinematic lighting, high-end fashion photography aesthetic, believable environmental integration.
```

## Low-Key Saturn Projection Portrait

> Sinematik, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "task_type": "low_key_projection_mapping_portrait",
      "language": "en",
      "priority": "highest",
      "version": "v1.0_SATURN_PROJECTOR_RAINBOW_LOWKEY"
    },
    "output": {
      "aspect_ratio": "4:5",
      "resolution": "high",
      "num_images": 4
    },
    "scene": {
      "concept": "a moody low-key portrait using projection mapping as the primary light source",
      "environment": "pure black seamless background, no visible room details, no props",
      "composition": "3/4 portrait, subject slightly low in frame with negative space above, Saturn positioned behind head like a luminous halo"
    },
    "subject": {
      "person": "adult woman",
      "wardrobe": {
        "top": "dark minimal top, non-reflective fabric to keep focus on projection"
      },
      "hair_makeup": {
        "hair": "long hair down, natural texture",
        "makeup": "minimal, realistic, preserve pores and skin texture"
      },
      "pose": "head slightly tilted, one hand near ear, relaxed shoulders",
      "expression": "calm, dreamy, subtle smile"
    },
    "projection": {
      "content": "glowing {argument name="planetary body" default="Saturn"} with rings",
      "placement": "Saturn centered behind head, ring arc extending to the side",
      "light_behavior": "rainbow refraction bands across face/arm, soft falloff into shadows",
      "notes": "keep projection edges believable; avoid flat sticker look"
    },
    "camera": {
      "shot": "projection-mapped portrait",
      "lens_mm": 50,
      "aperture": 2.0,
      "focus": "sharp on eyes and lips; gentle falloff on hair",
      "quality_tags": "photoreal, cinematic, subtle film grain"
    },
    "lighting": {
      "style": "projector as key light + very subtle fill",
      "notes": "deep blacks, high contrast, no visible background spill, preserve natural skin texture and catchlights"
    },
    "color_grade": {
      "palette": "deep blacks, saturated cyan/magenta/green rainbow bands, cool planetary blues",
      "contrast": "high with controlled highlights",
      "effects": "subtle grain, slight halation on brightest projection areas"
    },
    "negative_prompt": "text, logo, watermark, bright background, HDR, plastic skin, over-smoothed face, cartoon, anime, CGI look, banding artifacts, crushed noisy shadows, extra fingers, deformed hands, distorted face"
  }
}
```

## Cinematic Industrial Portrait with Macro Perspective

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic, cinematic editorial portrait with extreme photorealism. Low-angle, macro-inspired perspective. The subject is positioned inside a dark, industrial circular tunnel or pipe, with wet, glossy surfaces reflecting light. One arm reaches forward toward the lens, fingers stretched and oversized in the foreground, commanding the frame. Dice lie scattered ahead with consistent scale and visual balance.
Wearing a vivid red jacket with precise tailoring, realistic fabric texture, natural folds, and uniform sheen. Hair is short, textured, and slightly messy, maintaining consistent volume and length. Black wraparound sunglasses with matching tint and shape obscure the eyes. Multiple metallic rings adorn the fingers.
Expression is cold, controlled, and confident, masculine presence, relaxed lips, no smile. Color grading is cool and moody with high contrast, deep shadows, and the red jacket acting as the primary visual accent. Metallic highlights and reflections enhance the industrial mood. Dramatic directional lighting prioritizes the hand, rings, and dice, creating strong depth and separation between foreground and background.
Overall tone is bold, aggressive, high-fashion, and surreal. Ultra-detailed skin realism, visible fabric grain, and sharply defined metal and dice surfaces. Shot as a high-end DSLR cinematic portrait at ultra-high resolution.
```

## Red Bull Commercial on Santorini Stairs

```
A photorealistic 4:5 vertical commercial-style image of a fit young woman (20–25) with a deep bronzed, sun-kissed tan and glistening wet-look skin, reclining diagonally on white Mediterranean-style stucco stairs (Santorini/Mykonos vibe). She has a toned, slim-muscular body with defined abs and long legs, bare feet with toes pointed on the bottom step. She wears a vibrant {argument name="bikini color" default="royal blue"} triangle bikini with high-cut minimalist bottoms. Her dark brown/black hair is slicked back in a wet look, away from her face.
She is in a relaxed, confident pose: legs extended diagonally and crossed at the ankles, right arm bent holding a {argument name="product name" default="Red Bull"} can to her lips as she drinks, left arm bent with her hand behind her head/neck. Her face is in a profile to three-quarter view, head slightly tilted back, eyes closed or heavily lidded, minimal natural makeup, straight refined nose, full nude-pink lips touching the can, high sculpted cheekbones, and a sharp jawline.
There are 7 Red Bull cans total: one in her hand, two on the step behind her left shoulder, one under her right thigh, two near her calves, and one on the bottom step near her feet. The white plaster wall has “Red Bull” embossed twice in stacked white-on-white relief. The scene uses stark white and deep cobalt/royal blue geometric accents behind the stairs.
Lighting is harsh mid-day sunlight with crisp diagonal shadows and high contrast between bright whites and deep blues, creating a summer, refreshing, energetic, luxurious mood. Shot like a high-end advertising campaign, diagonal composition, sharp focus on model and product, deep depth of field, 8K, hyper-detailed.
```

## Surreal Giant Woman Over Tokyo Shibuya Crossing

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A surreal, hyper-realistic urban scene of a colossal giant blonde Caucasian woman in her 20s towering over Tokyo’s Shibuya Crossing. She has fair glowing skin, soft rounded cheeks, full natural pink lips, a small straight nose, and light blue eyes. Her long blonde hair is loose, slightly windswept, and flowing naturally. She wears bold, stylish fashion: a fitted modern crop top in a white or metallic tone, an open leather or satin jacket draped stylishly, and high-waisted leather pants or a mini skirt for a confident high-fashion street-style look. Minimal luxury jewelry adds an elegant touch. She is posed like a giant resting over the city — leaning forward with both elbows planted on the rooftops of two modern glass skyscrapers, hands supporting her chin and jawline, upper body weight resting on the buildings. Her head is slightly tilted downward and her eyes are clearly looking down at the streets below, calmly observing the tiny city beneath her with a confident, stoic, dominant expression. She is not looking at the camera. Below her is the famous Shibuya scramble crossing with clear white diagonal stripes, tiny pedestrians crossing, miniature buses, taxis, and cars driving on the left side. An elevated silver commuter train with a green stripe passes through the mid-ground. Surrounding buildings are dense with Japanese billboards and signage. The sky is clear bright blue in mid-day sunlight. Lighting is natural hard sunlight with realistic shadows cast onto buildings and streets. Shot from a high drone angle looking down at the crossing but at eye-level with the giant woman. Hyper-realistic style with seamless integration between the giant subject and the miniature city. Aspect ratio 4:5.
```

```
Photorealistic full-length portrait of an elegant young woman, around 22 years old, with fair glowing skin and soft medium-brown hair flowing naturally in a gentle summer breeze. She is standing gracefully in a classic English country garden at golden hour, bathed in warm sunset light with soft sun rays filtering through lush greenery. She is wearing a feminine pastel pink cotton sundress with a delicate floral print, sleeveless design, square neckline, fitted bodice accentuating the waist, and a flowing flared skirt with a subtle ruffled hem ending at mid-calf. A simple, elegant pearl necklace rests at her collarbone, adding a timeless, romantic touch. Her makeup is natural and refined: luminous skin, subtle rosy blush, soft neutral eyeshadow, lightly defined lashes, and natural pink lips. She has a gentle, dreamy smile and is looking directly at the camera with a calm, confident expression, one hand lightly touching her hair in a candid, effortless pose.
She is surrounded by tall blooming {argument name="flower type" default="pink hydrangeas"} in the foreground, with neatly trimmed deep-green boxwood hedges framing the scene. In the background, a charming English countryside brick cottage with a traditional thatched roof appears softly blurred, creating depth and atmosphere. The overall mood is dreamy, romantic, and serene, evoking a classic European summer aesthetic. Shallow depth of field with creamy bokeh, ultra-realistic skin texture, highly detailed fabric folds, lifelike lighting and shadows. Shot as a high-end fashion and lifestyle photograph on Canon EOS R5, 85mm lens, f/1.8, cinematic composition, natural color grading, ultra-sharp focus, hyper-realistic, editorial-quality photography, 8K resolution.
```

surrounding the central figure are multiple human silhouettes positioned in the foreground and background, forming a loose semicircle around the subject; these silhouettes are rendered as nearly solid black shapes with minimal visible detail, appearing as upright, suit-wearing forms; two silhouettes stand behind the central figure at shoulder height, slightly left and right, while additional silhouettes occupy the lower foreground, their heads and shoulders partially blocking the lower part of the frame; these surrounding figures are front-facing but remain indistinct, with no facial features visible, creating a layered depth effect with foreground silhouettes, the central subject in the midground, and additional silhouettes in the background; the background itself is a smooth, light gray or off-white plane with no visible texture or environmental context, suggesting a studio-like setting; the camera perspective feels normal to slightly telephoto, compressing the distance between the central figure and the surrounding silhouettes; focus is sharp on the subject’s face, sunglasses, and clothing, while the silhouettes remain uniformly dark and detail-less, emphasizing contrast rather than depth of field blur; lighting is directional from above and slightly to the front, illuminating the central subject’s face and blazer while leaving the surrounding figures in deep shadow; shadows are soft on the central subject but extremely dense on the silhouettes, creating a high-contrast, graphic effect; subtle specular highlights appear along the hair and the glossy surfaces of the sunglasses; ## Triptych Composition Prompt for Abstract Fashion Editorial

> Moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
THE TASK
Act as a world-class fashion photographer shooting a defining editorial campaign. Create a cohesive 3-panel vertical triptych (stacked top, middle, bottom) of the same subject. The sequence escalates intimacy and intensity through extreme proximity, revealing tactile hyper-realism and restrained dynamic power purely via photographic technique — no environmental changes, no added elements.
TRIPTYCH COMPOSITION
(VERTICAL STACK – ESCALATING CLOSE-UP INTENSITY)
Top Panel – Presence (Intense Dominant Close-Up)
Dynamic low 3/4 angle close-up (camera below the main form, tilted upward) to create looming, commanding scale.
Tight crop: the subject’s primary focal feature and sharp contours dominate; one side falls into deep shadow.
Subtle implied motion via slight rotational tilt and micro-blur only on far-edge highlights — keep the central zone razor-sharp.
Specular streaks rake across glossy surfaces like wet lacquer, emphasizing curvature and fine material imperfections.
Background: pure high-key white with faint gradient falloff — identical across all panels, no environmental cues.
Middle Panel – Control (Kinetic Structural Sweep)
Canted-angle macro from the side with aggressive perspective distortion.
Crop frames the central structural zone, with key elements off-center and partially cut by frame edges.
Highlights slice diagonally across contoured channels and ridges due to the dramatic angle.
Very shallow depth of field: nearest surface tack-sharp, farther planes melt softly to suggest velocity and tension.
Same pure high-key white background and exact tonal range — variation achieved solely through camera angle and lens proximity.
Bottom Panel – Essence (Extreme Graphic Detail)
Extreme macro from an aggressive angle evoking a controlled whip-pan. Choose one signature detail and make it feel alive with implied motion while remaining graphically pristine:

A protruding or extending element shot from below, strongly foreshortened, reaching toward the lens,
Or a central geometric/functional feature shot on a steep diagonal, highlights spiraling like motion trails,
Or a sharp edge or blade-like contour shot from behind, catching a razor-thin specular line.

Apply controlled directional blur only to a secondary trailing plane; keep the primary micro-texture impeccably crisp.
Frame so tight it becomes near-abstract: beveled edges, micro-scratches, gloss pooling, subtle material stress marks, fine imperfections.
No debris, no additional elements — identical high-key white background maintained.
STYLING & BOLD CHOICES (CRITICAL)
Subject: {argument name="subject material" default="any high-gloss, sculpted form with precise contours and reflective surfaces"}.
No additional objects, characters, props, text, or logos.
All sense of energy and motion derived exclusively from camera technique: low/canted angles, foreshortening, shallow depth of field, controlled selective blur, and highlight flow.
Identical lighting rig across all three panels — consistent
```

```
Ultra-Realistic Promotional
Surreal minimal beauty composition on a soft {argument name="background color" default="peach"} background with a pink watercolor texture at the top. Two elegant female hands painted in a matte nude-beige color, forming a symmetrical curved shape framing empty space. Floating above the hands are sunglasses. Below the sunglasses: glossy metallic gold lips, floating without a face. High-fashion editorial style, clean lighting, smooth shadows, aesthetic surrealism, pastel tones, elegant composition, luxury product photography mixed with artistic collage.
```

## Dark Fantasy Product Shot of Monster Energy Drink

```
{
[Subject: {argument name="product" default="Monster Energy Drink Can"}] [Details: Matte black finish, neon green 'M' claw logo, ultra-realistic condensation beads] [Environment: Dark basalt terrain, ethereal green toxic mist, volumetric lighting] [Background: Blurred silhouette of a demonic entity, dark forest atmosphere] [Style: Product Photography + Dark Fantasy Art] [Technical: 8k, Unreal Engine 5 render, ray-tracing, depth of field f/2.8, high-speed shutter effect] [Color Grading: High contrast, monochromatic black vs radioactive green]
```

## 9-Panel Portrait Photo Grid Prompt

> Sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
In a soft beige studio, construct a 3x3 fine-edited photo grid.

The character wears a light, thin {argument name="top color" default="dark blue"} shirt, {argument name="pants color" default="off-white"} trousers, and is barefoot to show raw texture.

Lighting design:
Large area soft light from the front right, silver reflector on the left, weak contour light from the top.

9 sets of fine lens compositions:
1. 85mm f/1.8 extreme lip close-up
2. Eye contact close-up
3. Black and white chin support portrait
4. Partially obscured portrait
5. Hand covering face close-up
6. Hair covering eye composition
7. Hand touching chin detail
8. Half-body side sitting pose
9. Cinematic side profile with tears
```

## Unplanned Paparazzi-Style Portrait Prompt

> Moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
An unplanned paparazzi-style portrait capturing a raw, candid moment. A tight close-up shows a woman walking through a crowded street, her head slightly lowered before glancing sideways toward the camera with a calm, confident expression. The frame includes only her face, neck, and part of her shoulder, creating an intimate and exclusive feel.
Shot at very close eye level, as if the photographer is moving within the crowd. The background is filled with blurred silhouettes of people, flashing cameras, and city lights smeared by motion, conveying the chaos of a fashion-week crowd.
Harsh on-camera flash illuminates her face, creating sharp highlights on the skin and stylish sunglasses. The image has a gritty high-ISO texture, cool-leaning tones, and strong contrast.
She wears the exact same outfit as in the reference photo, with no changes in color or detail. Her hair is slightly tousled by movement, enhancing the spontaneous, unpolished feel.
The overall mood is raw, fast, and authentic—effortless street-style paparazzi energy with a European fashion-week atmosphere, glamorous yet completely unplanned.
Aspect ratio 9:16.
```

## Ultra-Realistic Beauty Editorial with Kiss Marks

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic beauty fashion editorial photograph, taking reference from the uploaded image (exact facial features, skin tone, makeup placement, hair color and texture, proportions, accessories).

A confident woman posed against a clean white studio background, wearing a red beret and oversized red heart-shaped sunglasses with translucent lenses. She lifts the sunglasses slightly with a red satin glove, gazing past the camera with a bold, expressive look.

Her makeup is dramatic and editorial: glossy deep red lips, defined brows, clean skin texture. Red lipstick kiss marks are stamped across her face, neck, shoulders, and upper chest — realistic transfer texture, uneven edges, natural opacity variation.

Soft studio lighting with gentle shadows, even exposure, accurate reds without oversaturation.

True-to-life skin texture with visible pores, natural highlights, realistic lipstick shine, authentic fabric folds in gloves and beret.

Shot on a full-frame DSLR, 85mm lens, f/4, beauty editorial realism, RAW photo look.

Feels like a real fashion magazine shoot confident, playful, provocative, not stylized or artificial.

NEGATIVE PROMPT:
AI-generated look, CGI, illustration, airbrushed skin, plastic texture, warped facial features, fake gloss, exaggerated highlights, symmetry artifacts, unreadable makeup texture, painterly style, HDR glow, uncanny valley, over-smoothing.
```

## Editorial Angle Reference Sheet Grid

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
High-resolution Editorial Angle Reference Sheet. Perfect 3x3 grid. Aspect ratio 4:5. Strictly use attached selfie as identity reference. Maintain 100% facial consistency across all 9 panels (same face, age, hair, skin tone, proportions). Wardrobe (fixed for all panels): Oversized red suit jacket and trousers, white dress shirt, black necktie. Clean editorial oversized fit. Background: neutral light-grey studio cyclorama. Lighting: soft professional studio lighting with subtle fill. Camera: 85mm portrait look, shallow depth of field, sharp focus on face and outfit. Composition: centered subject in every cell, consistent framing, balanced spacing. Grid labels: clean white sans-serif text at bottom center of each cell showing the angle. Row 1 (Top): 1. Front Medium — front-facing waist-up, direct gaze. 2. Close-Up — extreme close-up on face and eyes. 3. Three-Quarter — 3/4 turn of torso and face. Row 2 (Middle): 4. Profile — exact side profile. 5. Low Angle Full — full body from slightly below. 6. High Angle — camera slightly above. Row 3 (Bottom): 7. Over Shoulder — over-the-shoulder look back. 8. Dutch Tilt — slight camera tilt. 9. Mid Walk — natural mid-step candid pose. Style: editorial fashion photography, cinematic, high-end. Render quality: ultra photorealistic, sharp text, visible fabric texture, natural skin, consistent lighting. Negative prompts: identity change, distorted anatomy, extra limbs, inconsistent clothing, busy background
```

```
Sharp oval face, deep-set almond eyes with a tired seductive gaze, thick natural brows, straight nose, slightly parted lips, light stubble beard and moustache. Jet-black slicked-back hair with natural volume.
Wearing a tailored charcoal-grey three-piece pinstripe suit, white spread-collar shirt, deep burgundy tie with gold tie bar, white pocket square, luxury metal watch. Silver ring on index finger.
Left hand in trouser pocket, right hand raised near lips holding a toothpick, relaxed yet dominant posture. Black ink neck tattoo visible, subtle finger tattoos.
Professional cinematic studio lighting: soft frontal key light, subtle fill, gentle rim light. Deep burgundy gradient background, smooth matte texture.
Warm cinematic color grading, shallow depth of field,  extreme realism, luxury fashion editorial,
```

```
Photorealistic, high-contrast portrait (9:16) of a young woman with short sleek black pixie-cut hair and pale skin, standing on a downward sloping Japanese urban street and looking back over her shoulder. Crisp facial detail and tattoos with shallow depth of field: a black ink leafy vine tattoo from the left neck to jawline and cheek, and a blackwork sleeve on the right arm with a mandala on the shoulder transitioning into fluid wave motifs on the forearm. She wears a black spaghetti-strap dress with an open back and high slit, holding a silver smartphone in her right hand with dark red manicured nails. Background: strong bokeh of beige mid-rise buildings, red anti-slip painted road lanes, a pink poster on a fence, blurred pedestrians, and warm storefront signage lights. Soft daylight, very subtle blue lens artifact in the upper right (not sunny blue-sky).
```

## Candid Paparazzi-Style Street Photograph

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
An ultra-realistic, photorealistic candid paparazzi-style street photograph of a joyful young blonde girl captured mid-laugh on a bright, sunny day. She is looking slightly off-camera, caught in a spontaneous, unposed moment, radiating happiness and carefree energy. Her expression is lively and genuine, eyes softly squinting from laughter, natural smile showing subtle teeth, with highly detailed facial features, realistic skin texture, visible pores, soft blush on cheeks, natural lips, and delicate highlights on her nose and cheekbones from direct sunlight.
She wears a light blue floral headscarf with delicate white and pastel flower patterns, loosely tied around her head, gently framing her face with a few wisps of blonde hair escaping in the breeze. Her outfit includes a fitted white cropped T-shirt with bold, clean black typography reading “LONDON NEVER SUMMER” across the chest, slightly stretched from movement, paired with high-waisted medium-wash blue denim jeans that hug the waist naturally. A tan leather belt with a classic gold buckle adds contrast, and a sleek gold wristwatch catches the sunlight with subtle reflections.
The photograph is taken outdoors in natural daylight, with warm sunlight creating soft shadows and glowing rim light around her hair and shoulders. The background is an urban street setting, softly blurred with shallow depth of field, hinting at sidewalks, buildings, and city movement without stealing focus. The image has a handheld camera feel, slight motion realism, authentic paparazzi energy, lifestyle fashion photography style, candid street vibe, natural color grading, realistic contrast, high dynamic range, cinematic yet spontaneous composition, 85mm lens look, crisp focus on the face, ultra-high resolution, editorial-quality realism, modern street fashion aesthetic.
```

## Retro 1960s Barbiecore Cinematic Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
ROLE: World-class commercial/editorial photographer. Create a photorealistic cinematic portrait in retro 1960s "Barbiecore" fashion.

SUBJECT: Use the exact person from the reference photo. Preserve facial structure, natural skin tone, and curvy proportions (no slimming). Expression: Flirtatious, confident, soft, natural smile.

STYLE: 1960s retro glamour, vintage fashion magazine cover. Monochromatic pink palette (bubblegum/powder pink), white, gold accents. Glossy, polished, cinematic depth.

COMPOSITION: Vertical 2:3, tight bust-up portrait, studio setting. Bright solid pink background. Subject sits on a powder-pink velvet chair, leaning slightly back. One hand near collarbone, other holding a vintage white-and-gold telephone receiver near face

WARDROBE & HAIR: Bubblegum pink satin off-shoulder mini dress with puff sleeves. Voluminous curls with pink rollers (getting-ready look). Pearl earrings.

LIGHTING: High-key soft warm beauty lighting, gentle specular highlights on lips/cheekbones, subtle shadows for dimension. Luminous, inviting tone.

CAMERA: 85mm portrait lens, slightly low angle, ultra-realistic textures (visible pores, no plastic smoothing), glossy satin and skin
```

## Four Prompts for Ultra-Realistic Female Portraits (Dubai, Floral, 90s, Western)

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Prompt 1:
"Ultra-realistic, 9:16 ratio, full body photo using the woman
reference image. A Dubai boulevard scene under bright midday sun. The Burj Khalifa towers clearly in the background. A stylish person strides across the street wearing a brown top jacket over it, slim trouser, designer sneakers, and oversized sunglasses, holding an iced latte. Intense sunlight creates crisp shadows on clean asphalt. High-contrast reflections on luxury cars and glass skyscrapers. Palm trees, traffic lights, and signage are sharply defined. Ultra-modern architecture dominates. Sharp focus. Shot on Sony A7R, 50mm f1.4. ISO 100. Shutter speed 1/800. Daylight white balance. Minimal post-processing. Cinematic framing. High realism. Aspect ratio 4:5. Preserve composition. Disposable camera flash effect. Soft warm halos on highlights. Subtle grain. Stylish lifestyle travel shot. the face expression remain same."

Prompt 2:
"Dreamy cinematic fashion editorial portrait of a young woman(match face) surrounded by blooming flowers at night, Petra Collins floral aesthetic, intimate feminine surrealism, emotional softness, poetic vulnerability. Shot on Canon EOS R5, 35mm f/1.4, shallow depth of field, creamy bokeh. Natural moonlight mixed with softbox diffusion, subtle overhead lens flare, warm vintage film tones, glowing skin, soft motion blur, floral foreground bokeh, analog grain. 
Vogue, Dazed editorial mood.
LENS: 35mm f/1.4
LUT: Petra Collins Pastel Film (Portra 400 warmth)
HARMONY: soft pink - midnight green warm beigesRef: Petra Collins x Sofia Coppola garden mood TOKENS: dreamy realism, floral intimacy, cinematic femininity.
HIDDEN TOKENS: dreamlike realism, emotional softness, analog soul, cinematic femininity, editorial vulnerability, poetic fashion, organic skin texture, subtle surrealism, imperfect beauty, tactile film grain, nostalgic youth, intimate gaze, soft realism.
NEGATIVE PROMPT: over-sharpened,
plastic skin, harsh HDR, over-processed, hyper contrast, CGI look, artificial lighting, fashion catalog stiffness, mannequin pose. AR: 3:4."

Prompt 3:
"A young woman with the same unchanged face, pale soft complexion, caught in a classic 90s disposable-camera snapshot. Her loose black hair falls naturally around her face. She gives a subtle smirk, relaxed and unbothered. She's wearing a simple white baby T-shirt with a fitted silhouette. The background is a light beige wall cluttered with polaroids, doodles, and taped-up concert tickets. The flash feels soft and airy with light grain."

Prompt 4:
"Ultra-realistic 8K Western Cinematic portrait of a stylish woman(match face) in a trendy floral sundress, full body playful cute pose, single consistent identity, face geometry locked. Lush botanical garden background, dappled sunlight, high-impact details, professional depth of field."
```

## Surrealist Ornithology fashion editorial prompt

```
{
  "vibe_title_en": "Surrealist Ornithology",
  "master_prompt": "A voyeuristic, long-lens capture peering through the clutter of a dimly lit, high-end taxidermy atelier. The Protagonist [gender-neutral, pale complexion, shaved head or slicked back hair] stands amidst shadows, wearing a structured white satin blazer. In a moment of surreal tension, they hold a hyper-realistic, oversized Flamingo Head Prop [crafted from painted porcelain and ethically sourced feathers] vertically against their face, creating a seamless 'split-face' illusion where the bird's beak aligns with the human jawline. The Protagonist casts a sharp, enigmatic glance directly at the camera with their one visible eye, creating a piercing connection. The composition is heavily occluded by out-of-focus foreground elements (antique glass jars, wireframes) to enhance the feeling of intrusion. Lighting is moody and cinematic: deep chiaroscuro shadows with a cool blue rim light striking the white satin and the flamingo's curve. Shot on a Hasselblad H6D-100c with a 210mm telephoto lens at f/3.2 to compress the space. The texture is distinctly analog, mimicking Cinestill 800T film with visible grain, ensuring the feathers and skin pores look tactile and physically grounded, rejecting any 'digital art' smoothness.",
  "meta": {
    "intent": "Editorial Fashion x Surrealism",
    "priorities": "Photographic Texture, Surreal Prop Integration, Voyeuristic Framing",
    "device_profile": "High-End Desktop / Large Format Print"
  },
  "frame": {
    "aspect": "9:16",
    "composition": "Voyeuristic framing with heavy foreground occlusion (peering through shelves/objects)",
    "layout": "Center-weighted subject but obstructed by negative space layers",
    "camera_angle": "Eye-level, shooting from a distance through a gap",
    "tilt_roll_degrees": "0"
  },
  "subject": {
    "gender": "Female",
    "identity": "The Protagonist",
    "demographics": "Androgynous High-Fashion Model, 20s-30s",
    "face": "Pale skin, sharp cheekbones, highly detailed skin texture/pores, one eye visible",
```

## Four actresses posing at a tropical beach

> Sinematik, minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A medium shot of four famous actresses, {argument name="actress 1" default="Ana de Armas"}, {argument name="actress 2" default="Millie Bobby Brown"}, {argument name="actress 3" default="Sadie Sink"}, and {argument name="actress 4" default="Sydney Sweeney"}, posing together on a sun-drenched tropical beach. They are arranged in a tiered group composition: Sadie Sink is on the left in the foreground making a peace sign and winking; Sydney Sweeney is kneeling in the front right smiling at the camera; Ana de Armas and Millie Bobby Brown stand behind them, smiling warmly.

Wardrobe & Styling
 * Outfits: Casual summer beachwear. Ribbed tank tops in vibrant primary colors (cobalt blue, bright yellow, crisp white, and black) tucked into light-wash high-waisted denim jeans and cargo-style denim pants.
 * Accessories: Minimalist gold jewelry, including small hoop earrings, thin chain necklaces, and several gold and silver wristwatches/bracelets.
 * Hair: Natural summer styles—Sadie with long wavy red hair; Sydney and Millie with sleek, slicked-back ponytails; Ana with soft, layered brunette hair.
Environment & Lighting
 * Setting: A pristine white sand beach with turquoise ocean waves gently lapping the shore in the background. Several tall, leaning coconut palm trees frame the top of the shot against a clear, pale blue sky.
 * Lighting: Bright, natural golden-hour sunlight coming from the side, creating soft highlights on their skin and a warm, glowing atmosphere.
 * Camera Style: Shot on a high-end digital camera (35mm lens), f/2.8 aperture for a slight soft-focus blur on the distant background, sharp focus on the subjects' faces, 8k resolution, cinematic color grading.
Technical Observations
While the image looks very convincing at a glance, it contains several classic AI artifacts that you might want to "prompt out" or fix in future versions:
 * Extra Fingers: Look closely at the hand on Sadie’s shoulder; there are extra digits blending into her hair.
 * Anatomical Merging: The way the arms and torsos overlap in the center is slightly physically impossible.
 * Watch Details: The watches have distorted faces/bands typical of AI-generated text and metal.
```

## High-Fashion Cinematic Portrait with Shutter Drag Effect

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"A high-fashion cinematic portrait of a woman with wavy auburn hair, wearing a deep-V neck black velvet gown. The scene is drenched in a monochromatic {argument name="lighting color" default="deep red"} studio light. A 'shutter drag' or long-exposure effect creates a ghostly, ethereal motion blur trailing to the right of her face. She has a poised, high-glance expression with bold red lipstick. One hand is gracefully touching her neck, showing a statement ring. Moody, dramatic shadows, 8k resolution, shot on 35mm lens, fashion editorial style."
>
Breakdown of Details
| Element | Description |
|---|---|
| Lighting | Saturated red monochromatic lighting with high contrast (Chiaroscuro effect). |
| Technical Effect | Shutter Drag / Motion Blur: The "ghosting" effect behind the head suggests movement during a slow exposure. |
| Subject | A woman with fair skin, freckles, and wavy {argument name="hair color" default="ginger/auburn"} hair. |
| Wardrobe | A minimalist, elegant black dress with a plunging neckline that contrasts sharply against the red background. |
| Composition | Medium shot, centered, with a soft-focus vignette that draws attention to the face and hands. |
Tips for Customizing
 * To change the mood: Replace "deep red" with "electric blue" or "neon emerald" for a different vibe.
 * To change the model: You can specify "shorter hair," "different ethnicity," or "alternative clothing" while keeping the "motion blur" and "monochromatic light" tags to maintain the style.
```
